CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_EMP`  (
  `ID` varchar(32) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `USERNAME` varchar(64) NULL COMMENT '姓名',
  `BIRTHDAY` date NULL COMMENT '出生年月',
  `AGE` int NULL COMMENT '年龄',
  `ORGID` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '科室编码',
  `ORGNAME` varchar(256) NULL COMMENT '科室名称',
  `MOBILE` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '手机',
  `SEX` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '性别',
  `FILTERTEST` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '是否过滤测试',
  `PHONE` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '联系电话',
  `NATION` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '民族',
  `STARTWORKDATE` date NULL COMMENT '参加工作时间',
  `TITLEDATE` date NULL COMMENT '职称聘用时间',
  `PROFESSIONALTITLE` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '专业技术职称',
  `POSITION` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '职务',
  `REMARK` varchar(512) NULL COMMENT '备注',
  `ELECTRONICSIGNATURE` varchar(256) NULL COMMENT '电子签名',
  `PIC` varchar(256) NULL COMMENT '照片',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME`  varchar(64) NULL COMMENT '制单人名称' ,
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT = '人员表'
;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_EMP_QUAL`  (
  `ID` bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `EMPID` varchar(32) NOT NULL COMMENT '用户名',
  `QUALNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证件名称/号',
  `QUALORG` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发证单位',
  `VALIDDATE` date DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '有效期',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称' ,
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`),
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_EMPQUAL_EMPID`(`EMPID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT = '人员-资格证书表'
;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_EMP_TEST`  (
  `ID` bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `EMPID` varchar(32)  NOT NULL COMMENT '用户名',
  `TESTID` bigint DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试编码',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称' ,
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`),
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_EMPTEST_EMPID`(`EMPID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT = '人员-测试表'
;

  `CAPACITY` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '容量',
  `COLOR` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '颜色',
  `MATERIAL` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '材质',
  `SAMPLEBOTTLECALIBER` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样瓶口径',
  `BOTTLENECKPROCESS` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '瓶口处理工艺',

  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8;

CREATE TABLE `t_emc_equipt_category`  (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `EQUIPTCATEGORY` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器类型名称',
  `EQUIPTCATEGORYDESC` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器类型描述',
  `MAINT` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否需要维护',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '仪器类型表' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

CREATE TABLE `t_emc_equipt_maint`  (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `EQUIPTCATEGORYID` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器类型ID',
  `MAINTEVENT` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '维护事件',
  `EVENTDESC` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '事件描述',
  `WARNINGDAYS` int(11) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '提前提醒',
  `SAMEDAY` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '计算当天',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_EM_EQUIPTCATEGORYID`(`EQUIPTCATEGORYID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '仪器维护事件表' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

CREATE TABLE `t_emc_org_permission`  (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `ORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位编码',
  `ORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位名称',
  `PERMISSIONTYPEID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '权限类型ID(业务表名)',
  `TARGETID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '目标权限ID',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_OP_PERMISSIONTYPEID`(`PERMISSIONTYPEID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_OP_TARGETID`(`TARGETID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '单位权限表' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

)
COMMENT='QC类型表';

  `departmentOrgName` varchar(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认科室名称',
  `postOrgId` varchar(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认岗位编码',
  `postOrgName` varchar(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认岗位名称',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',

  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic COMMENT='测试类型表';

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_UNIT`  (
  `ID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NOT NULL COMMENT '主键ID',
  `UNITCATEGORY` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位类型',
  `UNITNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位名称',
  `UNITFULLNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位全称',
  `UNITSHORTNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位简称',
  `STANDARDUNIT` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否标准单位',
  `BASICUNIT` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否基础度量单位',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` date DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_UNITCONVERT`  (
  `ID` bigint(32) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键ID',
  `UNITID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位编码',
  `CONVERTFACTOR` decimal(15, 2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '转换因子',
  `CONVERTUNITID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '转换单位',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` date DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_UNITCONVERT_UNITID`(`UNITID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

  `SAMPLE` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否采样',

  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8;

CREATE TABLE `t_emc_monitor_point_category`  (
  `ID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NOT NULL COMMENT '监测点类型编码<主键>',
  `MONITORPOINTTYPE` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点类型',
  `MONITORAREATYPE` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测区域类型',
  `MONITORTMPLGROUPID` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板组',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '监测点类型表' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

CREATE TABLE `t_emc_mpc_tmpl`  (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `MONITORPOINTCATEGORYID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点类型编码',
  `MONITORTMPLID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板编码',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_MT_MPCID`(`MONITORPOINTCATEGORYID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '监测模板表' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

-- ----------------------------- 2019-09-11 add by yuanhh--------------begin
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_METHOD` (
  `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `METHODNAME` VARCHAR ( 64 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法名称',
  `ACTIVATEDFLAG` VARCHAR( 1 )  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用标志',
  `ACTIVATEDBYID` VARCHAR( 32 )  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人编码',
  `ACTIVATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR( 64 )  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
  `ACTIVATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR ( 32 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR ( 64 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR ( 32 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR ( 256 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY ( `ID` )
) ENGINE = INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET = utf8 COMMENT = '方法';

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION` (
  `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
  `VERSIONNO` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '版本号',
  `TESTMETHODBASIS` VARCHAR ( 64 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检测方法及依据',
  `BEGINDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开始日期',
  `EXPIREDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '过期日期',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR ( 32 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR ( 64 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR ( 32 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR ( 256 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY ( `ID` ),
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_METHODVERSION_METHODID`(`METHODID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET = utf8 COMMENT = '方法版本';

COMMIT;

) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

DROP TABLE T_EMC_CONTAINER_CATEGORY;

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_CONTAINER_CATEGORY` (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '容器类型编码',
  `CONTAINERCATEGORYNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '容器类型名称',
  `CAPACITY` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '容量',
  `COLOR` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '颜色',
  `MATERIAL` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '材质',
  `SAMPLEBOTTLECALIBER` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样瓶口径',
  `BOTTLENECKPROCESS` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '瓶口处理工艺',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `REMARK` varchar(512) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8;


DROP TABLE `T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY`;

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY` (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `BIZCATEGORYID` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务类型编码',
  `BIZCATEGORYNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务类型名称',
  `REPORTNAME` varchar(128) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告名称',
  `TYPE` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '类别',
  `SAMPLE` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否采样',
  `SAMPLEORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认采样科室编码',
  `REPORTEDITORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认报告编制科室编码',
  `REPORTSENDORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告发送科室编码',
  `ENTRUSTORG` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认委托单位名称',
  `TESTORG` varchar(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认受检单位名称',
  `NDCAL` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'ND计算方式',
  `PROJNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认项目名称',
  `ENCRYPTION` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否加密',
  `EDITMONITORINFO` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '编辑监测点信息',
  `REPORTWORD` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认报告字',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` timestamp NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8;

 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=UTF8 COMMENT='区域表';

  `ACTIVATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
  `ACTIVATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_ORDER_INDEX_ID`(`indexCategoryId`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

  `indexName` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标名称',
  `index1` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标1',
  `index2` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标2',
  `index3` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标3',
  `index4` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标4',
  `index5` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标5',
  `index6` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标6',
  `reportDisplayName` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告中显示名称',
  `ACTIVATEDFLAG` varchar(1) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用标志',
  `ACTIVATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人编码',
  `ACTIVATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
  `ACTIVATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_ORDER_INDEX_ID`(`indexCategoryId`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `TESTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试编码',

     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `ANALYSISORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析科室编码',
     `METHODPARAM` VARCHAR(128)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法参数',
     `CONROLLEDNO` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析记录受控编号',
     `DISPLAYMONITORBASIS` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '显示监测依据',
     `AUTHENTICATION` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否认证',
     `APPROVAL` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否认可',
     `ANALYSISRECORDNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析记录名称',
     `ANALYSISFEE` DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析费',
     `SAMPLEFEE` DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样费',
     `HOURS` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '时效(小时)',
     `DEFAULTFLAG` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认方法',
     `ACTIVATEDFLAG` VARCHAR( 1 )  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用标志',
     `ACTIVATEDBYID` VARCHAR( 32 )  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人编码',
     `ACTIVATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR( 64 )  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
     `ACTIVATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='测试方法';

 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='测试方法分析项';

-- -----------------------------2019-10-03 add by magh--------------begin
-- ----------------------------
-- Table structure for t_emc_project
-- ----------------------------
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `t_emc_project`;
CREATE TABLE `t_emc_project`  (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `PROJNO` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编号',
  `PROJNAME` varchar(128) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目名称',
  `TESTORGID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '受检单位编码',
  `TESTORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '受检单位名称',
  `PROJADDRESS` varchar(128) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目地址',
  `ENTRUSTORGID` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '委托单位编码',
  `ENTRUSTORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '委托单位名称',
  `ENTRUSTORGADDRESS` varchar(128) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '委托单位地址',
  `BIZCATEGORYID` bigint(11) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务类型编码',
  `TASKNO` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '任务编号',
  `REGISTDATE` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '登记日期',
  `REPORTORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告科室编码',
  `ACCEPTDATE` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '受理日期',
  `COMPLEDATE` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目完成期限',
  `ENTRUSTDATE` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '委托日期',
  `SENDDATE` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '送样日期',
  `SENDTYPE` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '送样方式',
  `ENTRUSTMETHOD` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '委托方式',
  `SUBPACKAGEDESC` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分包说明',
  `NDCAL` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'nd计算方式',
  `SENDUSERNAME` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '送样人',
  `TASKDATE` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '任务日期',
  `TESTCONTENT` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检测内容及要求',
  `QCREQ` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '质控要求',
  `REMARK` varchar(512) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
  `PROCESSSTATUS` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',

    `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT 'ID',
     `EMPID` VARCHAR(32)  NOT NULL COMMENT '人员编码',
     `CERTNO` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书编号',
     `CERTNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书名称',
     `AUTHDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '授权日期',
     `EXPIREDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '过期日期',
     `WARNINGDAYS` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '提前提醒（天）',
     `STATUS` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '状态',
     `ORDERNO` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '排序码',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='人员证书表';

     `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '联系人编码',
     `CERTID` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书ID',
     `TESTID` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试编码',
     `METHODID` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(512)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='方法内容表';

 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='审核历史表';

-- 创建文件类型表 T_EMC_FILE_CATEGORY
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `T_EMC_FILE_CATEGORY`;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_FILE_CATEGORY` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `FILECATEGORYNO` VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件类型编码',
  `FILECATEGORYNAME` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件类型名称',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='文件类型表';

-- 创建文件表 T_EMC_FILE
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `T_EMC_FILE`;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_FILE` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `FILENO` VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件编号',
  `FILENAME` VARCHAR(128) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件名称',
  `FILECATEGORYID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件类型',
  `ARCHIVEORGID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '归档科室',
  `ARCHIVENAME` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '归档文件柜号及文件夹名称',
  `REGISTERDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '登记时间',
  `REGISTERUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '登记人',
  `DEPARTMENTUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '科室负责人',
  `LOC` VARCHAR(128) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件存放位置',
  `AUDITUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核人',
  `APPROVEUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '批准人',
  `APPROVEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '批准发布日期',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='文件表';

-- 创建文件版本表 T_EMC_FILE_VERSION
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `T_EMC_FILE_VERSION`;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_FILE_VERSION` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `FILEID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件编码',
  `VERSIONNO` VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '版本编号',
  `APPLYUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '申请人',
  `USEDUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件使用人',
  `OPERATIONTYPE` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作类型',
  `MODIFYREASON` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '更改替换理由',
  `BEFOREMODIFYCONTENT` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改/置换前内容',
  `AFTERMODIFYCONTENT` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改/置换后内容',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`),
  KEY `IDX_EMC_FV_FILEID` (`FILEID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='文件版本表';

-- 创建文件分发回收记录表 T_EMC_FILE_VERSION_DIST
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `T_EMC_FILE_VERSION_DIST`;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_FILE_VERSION_DIST` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `FILEVERSIONID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件版本编码',
  `CONTROLLED` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否受控',
  `DISTNO` VARCHAR(16) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发放编号',
  `OPERATIONTYPE` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作类型',
  `APPROVERUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '批准人',
  `REQDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '领用日期',
  `REQUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '领用人',
  `RECYCLEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '回收时间',
  `RECYCLEUSERID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '回收人',
  `RECYCLESTATE` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '回收状况',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`),
  KEY `IDX_EMC_FVD_FILEVERSIONID` (`FILEVERSIONID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='文件分发回收记录表';

  `LOWLIMIT` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出低限',
  `HIGHLIMIT` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出高限',
  `INDEXLOWLIMIT` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标低限',
  `INDEXHIGHLIMIT` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标高限',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_OTR_PROJID`(`PROJID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_OTR_FOLDERID`(`FOLDERID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_OTR_ORDERID`(`ORDERID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_OTR_ORDERTASKID`(`ORDERTASKID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '样品结果' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

-- Table structure for t_emc_ot_item_group
-- ----------------------------
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `t_emc_ot_item_group`;

  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `TESTMETHODID` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法编码',
  `ITEMGROUPID` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项组编码',
  `ITEMGROUP` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项组',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `ORDERID` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编码',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_OIG_ORDERID`(`ORDERID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '样品测试分析项组' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

INSERT INTO T_EMC_TEST(ID,TESTNAME) (SELECT (@i:= @i+1) as rank,TESTNAME FROM (select TESTNAME from t_emc_test_method group by TESTNAME) aaa, (SELECT @i:=0) as i);

update T_EMC_TEST_METHOD A set A.TESTID=(select id from T_EMC_TEST where TESTNAME = A.TESTNAME);

     `METHODPARAM` VARCHAR(66)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法参数',

     `ANALYSISUSERNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析员',

 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=UTF8 COMMENT='样品测试批表';

drop table T_EMC_ORDER;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_ORDER`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `ORDERNO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编号',
     `PROJID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
     `FOLDERID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编码',
     `LABELNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标签编号',
     `CLIENTORDERNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户样品名称',
     `PARENTORDERNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '父样编号',
     `ORDERDESC` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品描述',
     `SENDDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '送样日期',
     `SENDTIME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '送样时间',
     `CYCLE` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '周期',
     `FREQUENCY` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '频次',
     `SAMPLEID` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样单编码',
     `SAMPLEBEGINDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样开始日期',
     `SAMPLEBEGINTIME` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样开始时间',
     `SAMPLECOMPLETEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样完成日期',
     `SAMPLECOMPLETETIME` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样完成时间',
     `SAMPLENUM` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样数',
     `QCCATEGORYID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC类型主键',
     `QCCATEGORYCODE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC类型编码',
     `QCNAME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC类型中文',
     `MONITORTMPLID` VARCHAR(18)  NOT NULL COMMENT '监测模板ID',
     `ORDERCATEGORY` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品类型',
     `ORDERNUM` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '排序码',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='样品';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_ORDER_PROJID ON T_EMC_ORDER (PROJID);

CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_ORDER_FOLDERID ON T_EMC_ORDER (FOLDERID);

drop table T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `FOLDERID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编码',
     `ORDERID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编码',
     `PROJID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
     `ORDERTASKID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品测试编码',
     `TESTMETHODID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法编码',
     `METHODID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `METHODVERSIONID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法版本编码',
     `ITEMID` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项编码',
     `ITEMORDERNO` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项排序码',
     `ITEMNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项',
     `ITEMALIASNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项别名',
     `REVISIONRULE` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修约规则',
     `UNITNAME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '计量单位',
     `REPEATNO` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '重复数',
     `REPEATQTY` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '重复项',
     `ORIGINALRESULT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '原始结果',
     `REPORTRESULT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报出结果',
     `REPORTDISPLAY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告中显示',
     `RESULTSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '结果状态',
     `ANALYSISTYPE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析类型',
     `LOWLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出低限',
     `HIGHLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出高限',
     `INDEXLOWLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标低限',
     `INDEXHIGHLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标高限',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='样品结果';

CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_OT_RESULT_ORDERID ON T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT (ORDERID);

CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_OT_RESULT_FOLDERID ON T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT (FOLDERID);

CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_OT_RESULT_PROJID ON T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT (PROJID);

CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_OT_RESULT_ORDERTASKID ON T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT (ORDERTASKID);

     `SAMPLEUSERNAME` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样人名称',

     `MONITORPOINTCATEGORY` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点类型',

CREATE TABLE `t_emc_sample`  (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `sampleNo` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样单号',
  `projId` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
  `projNo` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编号',
  `orderCategory` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品类型',
  `remark` varchar(512) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
  `propertyTmplId` bigint(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '属性模板编码',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `PROCESSSTATUS` varchar(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_ORDER MODIFY SAMPLEID BIGINT;
commit;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_ORDER`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `ORDERNO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编号',
     `PROJID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
     `FOLDERID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编码',
     `LABELNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标签编号',
     `CLIENTORDERNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户样品名称',
     `PARENTORDERNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '父样编号',
     `ORDERDESC` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品描述',
     `SENDDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '送样日期',
     `SENDTIME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '送样时间',
     `CYCLE` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '周期',
     `FREQUENCY` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '频次',
     `SAMPLEID` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样单编码',
     `SAMPLEBEGINDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样开始日期',
     `SAMPLEBEGINTIME` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样开始时间',
     `SAMPLECOMPLETEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样完成日期',
     `SAMPLECOMPLETETIME` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样完成时间',
     `SAMPLENUM` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样数',
     `QCCATEGORYID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC类型主键',
     `QCCATEGORYCODE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC类型编码',
     `QCNAME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC类型中文',
     `MONITORTMPLID` VARCHAR(18)  NOT NULL COMMENT '监测模板ID',
     `ORDERCATEGORY` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品类型',
     `ORDERNUM` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '排序码',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='样品';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_ORDER_PROJID ON T_EMC_ORDER (PROJID);

drop table T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `FOLDERID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编码',
     `ORDERID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编码',
     `PROJID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
     `ORDERTASKID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品测试编码',
     `TESTMETHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法编码',
     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `METHODVERSIONID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法版本编码',
     `ITEMID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项编码',
     `ITEMORDERNO` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项排序码',
     `ITEMNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项',
     `ITEMALIASNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项别名',
     `REVISIONRULE` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修约规则',
     `UNITNAME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '计量单位',
     `REPEATNO` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '重复数',
     `REPEATQTY` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '重复项',
     `ORIGINALRESULT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '原始结果',
     `REPORTRESULT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报出结果',
     `REPORTDISPLAY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告中显示',
     `RESULTSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '结果状态',
     `ANALYSISTYPE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析类型',
     `LOWLIMIT`  DECIMAL(15,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出低限',
     `HIGHLIMIT` DECIMAL(15,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出高限',
     `INDEXLOWLIMIT` DECIMAL(15,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标低限',
     `INDEXHIGHLIMIT` DECIMAL(15,2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标高限',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='样品结果';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_CLIENT`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `CLIENTNO` VARCHAR(15)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户编码',
     `CLIENTNAME` VARCHAR(258)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户名称',
     `CLIENTCATEGORY` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户类别',
     `CLIENTSCATEGORY` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户小类',
     `AREAID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '区域编码',
     `ADDRESS` VARCHAR(513)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '地址',
     `PHONE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '电话',
     `WEBSITE` VARCHAR(129)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '网址',
     `MAIL` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '邮政信箱',
     `SCALE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '企业规模',
     `CREDITCODE` VARCHAR(15)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '统一社会信用代码',
     `LONGITUDE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '经度',
     `LATITUDE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '纬度',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(513)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(66)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(258)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='客户';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_CLIENT_CONTACT`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `CLIENTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户编码',
     `LXRNAME` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系人名称',
     `LXRPHONE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系人电话',
     `LXREMAIL` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系人邮箱',
     `FAX` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '传真',
     `POSTCODE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '邮编',
     `LXRADDRESS` VARCHAR(513)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系人地址',
     `JOB` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系人职务',
     `PROVINCE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '省',
     `CITY` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '市',
     `COUNTY` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '区',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(513)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(66)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(258)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='客户联系人';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_CC_CLIENTID ON T_EMC_CLIENT_CONTACT (CLIENTID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `CLIENTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户编码',
     `MONITORPOINTNO` VARCHAR(15)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点编码',
     `MONITORPOINTNAME` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点名称',
     `MONITORPOINTCATEGORYID` VARCHAR(15)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点类型编码',
     `MONITORPOINTTYPE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点类别',
     `MONITORPLACE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测地点',
     `INDEXID` VARCHAR(15)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品指标id',
     `EXPORT` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否出口',
     `MONITORPOINTLEVEL` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点级别',
     `CONTRASTFACTOR` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '编码对照因子',
     `LONGITUDE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '经度',
     `LATITUDE` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '纬度',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(513)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(66)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(258)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='监测点';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_MP_CLIENTID ON T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT (CLIENTID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT_SCHEME`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `MONITORPOINTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点编码',
     `MONITORTMPLGROUPID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板组ID',
     `MONITORTMPLID` VARCHAR(15)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板ID',
     `SCHEMEID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测方案ID',
     `SCHEMENAME` VARCHAR(66)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测方案名称',
     `ITEMGROUPID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项组编码',
     `ITEMGROUP` VARCHAR(66)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项组',
     `TESTMETHODID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法ID',
     `METHODID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(66)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(33)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(258)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='监测点方案';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_MPS_MONITORPOINTID ON T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT_SCHEME (MONITORPOINTID);

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_REPORT`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `PROJID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
     `MONITORTMPLID` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板ID',
     `ORDERCATEGORY` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品类型',
     `REPORTCONFIGLINEID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报表配置编码',
     `MONITORINFO` TEXT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测信息',
     `TESTCONCLUSION` TEXT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检验结论',
     `WORKCONDITION` TEXT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '工况',
     `REMARK` TEXT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `STATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '状态',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(18) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='报告表';
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_REPORT_RESULT`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `REPORTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告编码',
     `FOLDERID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编码',
     `ORDERID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编码',
     `PROJID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
     `ORDERTASKID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品测试编码',
     `FOLDERNO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编号',
     `ORDERNO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编号',
     `SAMPLEBEGINDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样开始日期',
     `SAMPLEBEGINTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样开始时间',
     `SAMPLECOMPLETEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样完成日期',
     `SAMPLECOMPLETETIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样完成时间',
     `MONITORTMPLID` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板ID',
     `MONITORPOINTNO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点编号',
     `MONITORPOINTNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点名称',
     `CLIENTORDERNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '客户样品名称',
     `SAMPLEID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样单编码',
     `ORDERDESC` VARCHAR(360)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品描述',
     `CYCLE` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '周期',
     `FREQUENCY` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '频次',
     `ORDERCATEGORY` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品类型',
     `SCHEMENAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方案名称',
     `TESTMETHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法编码',
     `TESTNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试名称',
     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `METHODNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法名称',
     `METHODVERSIONID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法版本编码',
     `ITEMID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项编码',
     `ITEMORDERNO` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项排序码',
     `ITEMNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项',
     `ITEMALIASNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项别名',
     `REVISIONRULE` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修约规则',
     `UNITNAME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '计量单位<存计量单位名称>',
     `REPEATNO` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '重复数（1,2,3...）',
     `REPEATQTY` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '重复项',
     `ORIGINALRESULT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '原始结果',
     `REPORTRESULT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报出结果',
     `RESULTSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '结果状态',
     `ANALYSISTYPE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析类型',
     `LOWLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出低限',
     `HIGHLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出高限',
     `INDEXLOWLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标低限',
     `INDEXHIGHLIMIT` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标高限',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='报告结果表';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_RR_REPORTID ON T_EMC_REPORT_RESULT (REPORTID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_RR_FOLDERID ON T_EMC_REPORT_RESULT (FOLDERID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_RR_ORDERID ON T_EMC_REPORT_RESULT (ORDERID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_RR_PROJID ON T_EMC_REPORT_RESULT (PROJID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_RR_ORDERTASKID ON T_EMC_REPORT_RESULT (ORDERTASKID);

-- 留样管理
-- 创建留样表 T_EMC_ORDER_RETAIN
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `T_EMC_ORDER_RETAIN`;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_ORDER_RETAIN` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `ORDERID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编码',
  `PROJID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目编码',
  `FOLDERID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编码',
  `RETAINQTY` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '留样数量',
  `RETAINTIMELIMIT` DATE DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '留样时限',
  `RETAINLOC` VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '留样位置',
  `RETAINUSERID` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '留样人',
  `RETAINDAYS` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '留样天数',
  `HANDLEUSERID` VARCHAR(30) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '处置人',
  `HANDLEDATE` DATE DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '处置日期',
  `HANDLERECORD` VARCHAR(120) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '处置记录',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人姓名',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位ID',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '创建人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='留样表';

  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',

) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='样品标签表';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_TRAIN_COURSE`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `COURSECODE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '课程编码',
     `COURSENAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '课程名称',
     `TRAINCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '培训类型',
     `TRAINCONTENTCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '培训内容类型',
     `TRAINHOUR` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '培训课时',
     `LECTURER` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '主讲人',
     `FEE` DECIMAL(10, 2) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '费用',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '摘要',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(258)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='培训课程';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_TC_TEST_METHOD`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `COURSEID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '课程ID',
     `TESTMETHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法ID',
     `TESTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试编码',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(258)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='涉及标准';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_TCTM_COURSEID ON T_EMC_TC_TEST_METHOD (COURSEID);

-- ----------------------------- 2019-10-30 add by qianc--------------begin
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_VENDOR`(
    `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `VENDORNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '厂商名称',
     `VENDORCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '厂商类型',
     `CONTACT` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系人',
     `PHONE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '电话',
     `EMAIL` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '邮箱',
     `FAX` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '传真',
     `POST` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '邮政编码',
     `ADDRESS` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '地址',
     `EVALUATIONLEVEL` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评价级别',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='生产商供应商';

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_VENDOR_MTL`(
     `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `VENDORID` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '厂商ID',
     `ORDERNO` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '排序码',
     `SUPPLYMTLNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '供应物品名称',

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_STD_SET_CATEGORY`(
    `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `STANDARDSET` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准设置',
     `STANDARDSETDESC` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准设置描述',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='标准设置分类';
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_STANDARD_SET`(
    `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `STANDSETCATEGORYID` BIGINT(20)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准设置分类id',
     `SETNAME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '设置名称',
     `SETDESC` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '设置描述',

     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='标准设置';

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-03 add by yuanhh--------------begin
UPDATE T_EMC_METHOD T
   SET T.MAXVERSIONID =
       (SELECT MAX(ID) FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE METHODID = T.ID GROUP BY METHODID),
       T.MAXVERSIONNO =
       (SELECT MAX(VERSIONNO) FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE METHODID = T.ID GROUP BY METHODID);

UPDATE T_EMC_METHOD T
   SET T.TESTMETHODBASIS =
       (SELECT TESTMETHODBASIS FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE ID=T.MAXVERSIONID),
       T.MAXELNTMPLID =
       (SELECT ELNTMPLID FROM T_EMC_METHOD_VERSION WHERE ID=T.MAXVERSIONID);
COMMIT;

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-7 add by zhanghang--------------begin

CREATE PROCEDURE `P_GETELNDSSQL_ORDERTASK`(IN IN_RUNID VARCHAR (32),OUT OUT_ELNSQL TEXT)
BEGIN
  DECLARE DONE BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0 ;
  -- 自定义变量
  DECLARE ELNSQL TEXT DEFAULT '';
    DECLARE ITEMALIASNAME VARCHAR(32);
    -- 声明游标：获取分析项
    DECLARE ITEMALIASNAMES CURSOR FOR
    SELECT DISTINCT OTR.ITEMALIASNAME
                 FROM T_EMC_ORDER_TASK OT, T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT OTR
                 WHERE OT.ID = OTR.ORDERTASKID AND OT.RUNID = IN_RUNID AND OTR.REPEATNO = 1;

  -- 设置结束标志
  DECLARE CONTINUE HANDLER FOR NOT FOUND SET DONE = 1;

SET ELNSQL = CONCAT(ELNSQL,'SELECT O.ORDERNO \'样品编号\', ');
  -- 打开游标
  OPEN ITEMALIASNAMES ;
        SQLLOOP:LOOP
            -- 批读取数据到指定变量上
            FETCH ITEMALIASNAMES INTO ITEMALIASNAME;
            -- 进行逻辑操作
            IF DONE = 1
                    THEN LEAVE SQLLOOP; -- 退出循环
            ELSE
                    SET ELNSQL = CONCAT(ELNSQL,'MAX(CASE OTR.ITEMALIASNAME WHEN \'', ITEMALIASNAME ,'\' THEN OTR.REPORTRESULT ELSE NULL END ) ',ITEMALIASNAME,',');
                    SET ELNSQL = CONCAT(ELNSQL,'MAX(CASE OTR.ITEMALIASNAME WHEN \'', ITEMALIASNAME ,'\' THEN OTR.ID ELSE NULL END ) ',ITEMALIASNAME,'_RID,');
            END IF;
    END LOOP;

        SET ELNSQL = SUBSTRING(ELNSQL, 1, CHAR_LENGTH(ELNSQL)-1 );
        SET ELNSQL = CONCAT(ELNSQL,' FROM T_EMC_ORDER_TASK OT,T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_RESULT OTR,T_EMC_ORDER O WHERE OT.ID = OTR.ORDERTASKID AND OT.ORDERID = O.ID AND OT.RUNID = \'' , IN_RUNID ,'\' AND OTR.REPEATNO =1 GROUP BY O.ORDERNO');

  -- 关闭游标
  CLOSE ITEMALIASNAMES ;
SELECT ELNSQL INTO OUT_ELNSQL ;
END

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-7 add by zhanghang--------------end

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_CONSUME`  (
  `ID` VARCHAR(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NOT NULL COMMENT '材料编码<主键>',
  `MTLNAME` VARCHAR(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '材料名称',
  `SPECIFICATION` VARCHAR(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '规格型号',
  `MTLCATEGORYID` BIGINT(10) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '材料类型',
  `HARMFULNESS` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '危害性',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `STOCKQTY` DECIMAL(15, 2) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '库存数量',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE
) COMMENT ='耗材表' ENGINE = INNODB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = DYNAMIC;

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_CSM_STOCK`  (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `MTLID` VARCHAR(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '材料ID',
  `BATCHNO` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '批号',
  `STOCKQTY` DECIMAL(15, 2) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '当前数量',
  `UNITNAME` VARCHAR(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '数量单位',
  `EXPIREDATE` DATE NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '失效日期',
  `LOWLIMIT` DECIMAL(15, 2) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '数量低限',
  `STATUS` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '状态',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_CSM_STOCK_MTLID`(`MTLID`) USING BTREE
) COMMENT ='材料库存表' ENGINE = INNODB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = DYNAMIC;


CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_CSM_STOCK_LOG`  (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `STOCKID` BIGINT(20) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '库存表ID',
  `LOGTYPE` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作类型 ',
  `PREVIOUSQTY` DECIMAL(15, 2) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作前数量',
  `QTY` DECIMAL(15, 2) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作后数量',
  `VENDORID` VARCHAR(16) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '供应商',
  `PRICE` DECIMAL(10, 2) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单价',
  `LOGCONTENT` VARCHAR(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注/描述',
  `RECEIVEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '领取人ID',
  `RECEIVEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '领用科室单位编码',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE,
  INDEX `IDX_EMC_CSM_STOCK_LOG_STOCKID`(`STOCKID`) USING BTREE
) COMMENT ='材料历史记录表' ENGINE = INNODB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = DYNAMIC;

) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='巡查记录表';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_CONVEN_MTL`(
    `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `MTLNAME` VARCHAR(72)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '物品名称',
     `SPECIFICATION` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '规格',
     `PRICE` DOUBLE(15,4)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单价',
     `QUANTITY` DOUBLE(15,2)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '数量',
     `TOTAL` DOUBLE(15,4)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合计',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `MODEL` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '型号',
     `LVL` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '等级',
     `VENDOR` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '供应商',
     `PURCHASEDATE` DATE  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '购买日期',
     `USEDORGNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '使用科室',
     `CHECKMETHOD` VARCHAR(72)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收标准和验收方法',
     `CHECKRECORD` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收记录',
     `CHECKRESULT` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收结论',
     `HANDLEOPINION` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '处理意见',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='常规物资';


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EQUIPT_PURCH`(
    `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `EQUIPTNAME` VARCHAR(72)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器名称',
     `MODEL` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '型号',
     `MANUFACTURER` VARCHAR(72)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '生产厂家',
     `PURCHLOC` VARCHAR(240)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '购买地点',
     `PURCHQTY` DOUBLE(15,2)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采购数量',
     `PRICE` DOUBLE(15,4)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单价',
     `PURCHATTACHNAME` VARCHAR(72)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '购买附件名称',
     `FEE` DOUBLE(15,4)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '经费',
     `PAYWAY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '付款方式',
     `TOTALAMOUNT` DOUBLE(15,4)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '总金额',
     `USEDESC` VARCHAR(1080)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '技术指标、用途说明',
     `FACTORYNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '出厂编号',
     `FACTORYDATE` DATE  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '出厂日期',
     `PURCHDATE` DATE  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '购置日期',
     `CONTRACTNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同编号',
     `AMOUNT` DOUBLE(15,4)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '金额',
     `CHECKRECORD` VARCHAR(1080)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调试验收记录',
     `CHECKRESULT` VARCHAR(72)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收结果',
     `CHECKGROUP` VARCHAR(72)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收小组',
     `DEBUGUSER` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调试验收人',
     `DEBUGDATE` DATE  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调试验收人验收日期',
     `CHECKRESPUSER` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收负责人',
     `CHECKRESPDATE` DATE  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收负责人验收日期',
     `RECEIVEUSER` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '接收使用人',
     `RECEIVEDATE` DATE  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '接收使用人验收日期',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器采购';

     `CHECKRESULT` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收结果',
     `CHECKUSER` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验收人',

     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='标准物质';

-- ----------------------------- 2019-11-13 add by cuikx--------------begin
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `T_EMC_CAR`;
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_CAR`  (
  `ID` bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `USEDEPTID` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '用车站室ID',
  `USEDEPTNAME` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '用车站室NAME',
  `CARNUM` varchar(12) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '车牌号',
  `PASSENGERIDS` varchar(300) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '随行人员Ids',
  `LEAVETIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '预计出发时间',
  `USEREASON` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '用车事由',
  `DESTINATION` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '目的地',
  `PICKCARSTATUS` varchar(1) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '取车状态',
  `REMARK` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
  `DRIVERId` varchar(30) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '驾驶人',
  `ACTUALUSETIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '实际用车开始时间',
  `ACTUALREBACKTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '实际用车结束时间',
  `BEFOREMILE` varchar(30) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '行驶开始里程',
  `AFTERMILE` varchar(30) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '行驶结束里程',
  `OILING` varchar(1) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否加油',
  `OIL` varchar(12) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加油量',
  `OILCARDNO` varchar(30) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '油卡号',
  `OILINGTIME` datetime(0) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加油时间',
  `OILINGMILE` varchar(30) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加油时里程',
  `PASSENGERNAMES` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '随行人员names',
  `drivername` varchar(30) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '驾驶人name',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(60) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(270) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `APPLYUSERID` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '申请人id',
  `APPLYUSERNAME` varchar(60) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '申请人名字',
  `ProcessStatus` varchar(30) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '汽车使用归还' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID,AUDITABLE,ATTACHMENT)
VALUES ('T_EMC_CAR', '1', '1');

        `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
        `ORGNAME` VARCHAR ( 30 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加班科室',
        `ORGID` VARCHAR ( 18 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加班科室id',
        `USERNAME` VARCHAR ( 30 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加班人员',
        `USERID` VARCHAR ( 18 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加班人员id',
        `BEGINTIME` DATETIME ( 1 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加班开始时间',
        `ENDTIME` DATETIME ( 1 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加班结束时间',
        `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR ( 64 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
        `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR ( 36 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
        `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR ( 60 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
        `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
        `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR ( 36 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
        `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR ( 270 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
        `REASON` VARCHAR ( 1080 ) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加班事项和原因',
        PRIMARY KEY ( `ID` )
) ENGINE = INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET = utf8 COMMENT = '加班表';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EQUIPT_DCU_CONF`(
     `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `ACQSCRIPTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采集脚本ID',
     `INTERFACECATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '接口类型',
     `BAUDRATE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '波特率',
     `TERMINATOR` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '终止字符串',
     `DATABIT` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '数据位',
     `PARITYCHECK` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '奇偶校验',
     `COM` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'COM口',
     `STOPBIT` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '停止位',
     `BEGINSYMBOL` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开始标记',
     `ENDSYMBOL` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '结束标记',
     `ANALYTICFORMULA` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '数据解析公式',
     `IP` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'ip地址',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='设备-DCU配置';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_FILE`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `EQUIPTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器ID',
     `SOURCEFILENAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '源文件名称',
     `TARGETFILENAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '目录文件名称',
     `CONTENT` longtext  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件文本内容',
     `RESULT` longtext  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '解析文件的字符串结果',
     `STATUS` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '处理状态0未处理，1已处理',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器采集文件';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_PARAM`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `FIELD` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '字段编码',
     `FIELDNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '字段名称',
     `ORDERNOFORMAT` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编号格式',
     `RESULTFORMAT` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件采集结果格式',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器采集参数配置';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EQUIPT_ACQ_RESULT`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `EQUIPTACQFILEID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器采集文件ID',
     `FILENAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采集文件名',
     `BIZNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务编号(样品编号/系统编号)',
     `ROWNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '行号',
     `FIELDNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '字段名称',
     `TESTNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试名称',
     `ITEMNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项名称',
     `RESULT` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '结果',
     `RESULTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '结果录入结果表的ID',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器采集结果';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EAS_PARAM`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `SCRIPTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器采集脚本ID',
     `PARAMID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '参数配置ID',
     `BEGINSYMBOL` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开始标记',
     `ENDSYMBOL` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '结束标记',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器采集脚本配置-采集参数';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_EPA_SCRIPTID ON T_EMC_EAS_PARAM (SCRIPTID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EAS_PREANALY`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `SCRIPTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器采集脚本ID',

     `PREANALYCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器采集脚本配置-预处理';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_EPR_SCRIPTID ON T_EMC_EAS_PREANALY (SCRIPTID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EAS_RESULT`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',

     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器采集脚本配置-结果参数';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_ER_SCRIPTID ON T_EMC_EAS_RESULT (SCRIPTID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_REPORT_QUERY`(
     `ID` VARCHAR(18)  NOT NULL COMMENT '申请编码',
     `QUERYCONTENT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '查用内容',
     `QUERYFORM` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '查用形式',
     `PURPOSE` VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '用途简述',
     `CONTACT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系人',
     `PHONE` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '联系电话',
     `SUBMITDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '要求报送时间',
     `REGISTER` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '登记人',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(240)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='数据报告查用';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_CURVE`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `CURVENO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '曲线编号',
     `FOLDERID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位编码',
     `EQUIPTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器编码',
     `TESTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试编码',
     `TESTNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试名称',
     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `METHODNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法名称',
     `ITEMID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项编码',
     `ITEMNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项名称',
     `INTERCEPT` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '截距',
     `SLOPE` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '斜率',
     `COEFFICIENT` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '相关系数',
     `CONFDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '配置日期',
     `EXPIREDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '过期日期',
     `MARKINGNO` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标液编号',
     `MARKINGNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标液名称',
     `MARKINGCONCENT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标液浓度',
     `MARKINGUNITNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标液单位',
     `MARKINGVALIDATE` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标液有效期',
     `MARKINGMANUFACTURER` VARCHAR(240)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标液生产单位',
     `USECONCENT` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '使用液浓度',
     `USECONCENTUNITNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '使用液浓度单位',
     `COLORVOLUME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '显色体积',
     `SOLCONF` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液配制',
     `CURVECONDITION` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '曲线绘制条件',
     `STATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '状态',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(256)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='曲线表';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_INFO_TMPL`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '模板编码',
     `TMPLNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '模板名称',
     `USEDORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '应用部门',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='上报模板';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL ADD PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '字段编码',
     `INFOTMPLID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '上报模板ID',
     `FIELD` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '字段名',
     `FIELDTITLE` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '字段标题',
     `WIDTH` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '宽度',
     `DISPLAY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '显示属性',
     `LOWLIMIT` DOUBLE DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出低限',
     `UNITNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位名称',
     `REVISIONRULE` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修约规则',
     `TRANSFORM` VARCHAR(3)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否转换列',
     `PRIMARYINDEX` INT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '主键列数',
     `PRIMARYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '主键名称',
     `BIZFIELD` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '对应系统字段',
     `DEFAULTVALUE` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '默认值',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='上报模板字段';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD ADD PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX ITF_INFOTMPLID ON T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_FIELD (INFOTMPLID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '测试编码',
     `INFOTMPLFIELDID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '上报模板字段id',
     `TESTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试代码',
     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `ITEMID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项编码',
     `MONITORTMPLID` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板编码',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='上报模板字段分析项';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM ADD PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX TFI_INFOTMPLFIELDID ON T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM (INFOTMPLFIELDID);
