INSERT INTO T_EMC_PROPERTY_DATA ( id, bizid, tmplid ) SELECT
F_GET_SEQ_CORE(),
A.ID,
B.MONITORPOINTTYPE
FROM
    T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT A
    LEFT JOIN T_EMC_MONITOR_POINT_CATEGORY B ON A.MONITORPOINTCATEGORYID = B.ID
    WHERE
    NOT EXISTS (
        SELECT TMPLID, BIZID FROM T_EMC_PROPERTY_DATA C WHERE C.BIZID = A.ID
    )

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_RESULT_LOG`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `ORDERTASKID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品测试id',
     `RESULTID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品测试结果编码',
     `REASON` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改结果原因',
     `PREVIOUSVALUE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改前结果',
     `VALUE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改后结果',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='样品测试任务结果修改记录表';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_RL_ORDERTASKID ON T_EMC_RESULT_LOG (ORDERTASKID);

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD`(
     `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `EQUIPTID` BIGINT(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '设备id',
     `MAINTID` BIGINT(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '维护事件id',
     `VERIFYORG` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检定机构',
     `CERTNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书编号',
     `CERTCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书类型',
     `CERTEFFECTDATE` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书生效日',
     `STATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '现在状态',
     `STARTSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开机状态',
     `AIRSATURATION` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '空气饱和度',
     `ADDICE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '加冰',
     `MAINTSITUATION` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '维护情况',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `COMPLETEFLAG` VARCHAR(1)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否完成',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器维护计划';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD_EQUIPTID ON T_EMC_EQUIPT_RECORD (EQUIPTID);

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_EQUIPT_PLAN`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `EQUIPTID` BIGINT(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器id',
     `MAINTID` BIGINT(32)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '维护事件id',
     `MAINTDATE` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '维护日期',
     `CYCLE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '维护周期',
     `WARNINGDAYS` BIGINT(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '提前提醒（天）',
     `ORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '科室编码',
     `MAINTENDDATE` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '维护到期日期',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_EQUIPT_PLAN PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='仪器维护记录';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_EQUIPT_PLAN_EQUIPTID ON T_EMC_EQUIPT_PLAN (EQUIPTID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_LOG`(
    `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `OPERATECATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '操作类型',
     `FOLDERID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '点位ID',
     `ORDERID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品ID',
     `ORDERTASKID` BIGINT  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品测试ID',
     `MODULE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '模板名称',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='样品测试流程记录表';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_OTL_ORDERTASKID ON T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_LOG (ORDERTASKID);

VALUES ('15000122','T_EMC_ORDER_TASK_LOG', 'T_EMC_ORDER_TASK', 'ORDERTASKID');

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS T_EMC_INFO_INDEX(
     ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '指标编码',
     INDEXNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标名称',
     INDEXVALUE VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '指标值',
     CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='评价标准表';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INFO_INDEX ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_INFO_INDEX PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS T_EMC_INFO_INDEX_CATEGORY(
     ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '标准分类编码',
     INDEXID BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评价标准编码',
     CATEGORY VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分类',
     REMARK VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='评价标准分类表';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INFO_INDEX_CATEGORY ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_INFO_INDEX_CATEGORY PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX INFO_INDEX_CATEGORY_INDEXID ON T_EMC_INFO_INDEX_CATEGORY (INDEXID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS T_EMC_CATEGORY_INDEX(
     ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '分类指标编码',
     INDEXCATEGORYID BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准分类编码',
     ORDERNO BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '排序码',
     ITEMNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项名称',
     LOWLIMIT DOUBLE DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '低限值',
     HIGHLIMIT DOUBLE DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '高限值',
     REVISIONRULE VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修约规则',
     DETECTLIMIT DOUBLE DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出限',
     CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='评价标准分类指标表';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_CATEGORY_INDEX ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_CATEGORY_INDEX PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_CI_INDEXCATEGORYID ON T_EMC_CATEGORY_INDEX (INDEXCATEGORYID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS T_EMC_CATEGORY_SECTION(
    ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '分类断面编码',
     INDEXCATEGORYID BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准分类编码',
     MONITORPOINTNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '断面名称',
     CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',

     `ID` BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `ASSETCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '资产类型',
     `ASSETNAME` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '资产名称',

     `QUANTITY` bigint(15)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '数量',

     `TOTALAMOUNT` double(15,4)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '总价金额',
     `PURCHASETIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '购入时间',
     `FACTORYNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '出厂编号',
     `RESPONSITYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '责任科室Id',
     `USEORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '使用科室Id',
     `USEMODIFY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '使用变更',

     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='固定资产管理表';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_ENV_RECORD`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `MONITORCOMPANY` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测公司',
     `ORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '部门编码',
     `ORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '部门名称',
     `ROOM` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '房间',
     `MONITORDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '日期',
     `MONITORTIME` VARCHAR(9) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '时间',
     `TEMPERATURE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '温度',
     `HUMIDITY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '湿度',
     `PRESSURE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '气压',
     `RECORDUSERID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '记录人编码',
     `RECORDUSERNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '记录人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='环境记录';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_ENV_RECORD ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_ENV_RECORD PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_BIOLOGY`(
     `ID` VARCHAR(36) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `PARENTID` VARCHAR(36)   NULL COMMENT '父id',
     `SPECIESCODE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '物种编码',
     `SPECIESNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '物种名字',
     `SPECIESENGLISHNAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '英文名字',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME   DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='生物项目分析';

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_TMPL_FIELD_ITEM MODIFY COLUMN `ITEMID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项编码';

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `INFOTMPLID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '模板编码',
  `MONITORPOINTID` BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点编码'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8;
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_INFO_TMPL_POINT_INFOTMPLID ON T_EMC_INFO_TMPL_POINT (INFOTMPLID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW`(
     `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `QUALITYCONTROLNO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '质控编号',
     `REVIEWPURPOSE` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审目的',
     `REVIEWSCOPE` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审范围',
     `REVIEWTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '拟计划评审时间',
     `PARTAKEUSER` VARCHAR(360)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '参与人员',
     `REVIEWADDRESS` VARCHAR(360)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审地点',
     `REVIEWWAY` VARCHAR(360)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审方式',
     `REVIEWCONTENT` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审内容',
     `PREPAREWORK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '准备工作',
     `REVIEWSTARTTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审开始时间',
     `REVIEWENDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审结束时间',
     `REVIEWBASIS` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审依据',
     `REVIEWDESC` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审情况描述',
     `ADVISE` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '整改措施及具体建议',
     `REVIEWOPINION` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审意见',
     `YEARTARGET` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '下一年度的目标和活动计划',
     `REVIEWGROUPUSER` VARCHAR(360)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审组成员',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='管理评审计划';

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID,AUDITABLE,ATTACHMENT) VALUES ('T_EMC_MANAGE_REVIEW', '1', '1');

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_QUALITY_CONTROL  (
  ID bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  QUALITYCONTROLCODE varchar(18) NULL COMMENT '质控编码',
  QUALITYCONTROLSCOPE varchar(36) NULL COMMENT '质控范围',
  QUALITYCONTROLCATEGORY varchar(36) NULL COMMENT '质控类型',
  QUALITYCONTROLOBJECT varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '质控对象',
  QUALITYCONTROLTARGET varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '质控目标',
  QUALITYCONTROLORGNAME varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '质控科室',
  REQUEST varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '要求',
  TIME varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '时间',
  REMARK varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '备注',
  STANDARDMTL varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '标准物质',
  TESTBASIS varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '测试依据',
  PARTAKEUSER varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '参加人员',
  TECHNICALREQUEST varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '技术要求',
  ENVCONDITION varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '环境条件',
  RESOURCECONFIG varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '资源配置',
  TESTORDER varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '测试样品',
  FEASIBILITY varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '计划可行性',
  REVIEWUSER varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '评审人员',
  EVALUATEUSER varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '评价人员',
  EVALUATEDATE date NULL COMMENT '评价日期',
  ORIGINALRECORD varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '原始记录',
  CONTROLRESULT varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '控制结论',
  INFLUENCE varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '影响因素',
  POTENTIALFACTOR varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '潜在因素',
  PRECAUTION varchar(540) NULL COMMENT '预防措施',
  PROCESSSTATUS varchar(36) NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
  CREATEDBYID varchar(36) NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  CREATEDBYNAME  varchar(60) NULL COMMENT '制单人名称' ,
  CREATEDTIME datetime NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  CREATEDBYORGID varchar(36) NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  CREATEDBYORGNAME varchar(270) NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=UTF8 COMMENT = '质量控制计划表';

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_PRECAUTION` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `PRECAUTECODE` VARCHAR(18) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '质控编号',
  `SOURCE` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '来源',
  `SOURCENO` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '来源编号',
  `IMPLEMENTORGNAME` VARCHAR(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '实施部门',
  `IMPLEMENTLEADER` VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '实施负责人',
  `PARTAKEUSER` VARCHAR(300) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '参加人员',
  `REASION` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '原因',
  `PRECAUTION` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '拟采取预防措施',
  `VERIFYMETHOD` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '拟采取验证方法',
  `COMPLETEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '预计完成时间',
  `VERIFYUSER` VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '验证人',
  `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='预防措施计划';

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `t_emc_internal_audit`;
CREATE TABLE `t_emc_internal_audit`  (
  `id` bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `qualityControlNo` varchar(18) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '质控编号',
  `auditPurpose` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核目的',
  `auditBasis` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核依据',
  `auditOrg` varchar(270) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '被审核部门',
  `auditGroupUser` varchar(360) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '内审组成员',
  `auditTime` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '拟计划审核时间',
  `auditContent` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '拟计划审核内容',
  `userTrain` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '内审人员培训（必要时）',
  `recordTable` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '记录表格',
  `startTime` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '内审开始时间',
  `endTime` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '内审结束时间',
  `auditDate` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核日期',
  `lastAuditDate` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '上次审核日期',
  `division` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核成员分工及审核部门',
  `auditSituation` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核情况综述',
  `inconformity` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '不符合项目归纳分类及分布项',
  `manageSystem` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '受审方的管理体系执行情况的整体综合评价',
  `correct` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '纠正措施或预防措施执行情况',
  `auditRequest` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核后续活动要求',
  `auditReview` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核综述',
  `sendScope` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发送范围',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
  PRIMARY KEY (`id`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

DROP TABLE IF EXISTS `t_emc_inconformity`;
CREATE TABLE `t_emc_inconformity`  (
  `id` bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `qualityControlNo` varchar(18) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '质控编号',
  `source` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '来源',
  `sourceNo` varchar(60) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '来源编号',
  `factDesc` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '不符合项事实描述',
  `inconformityBasis` varchar(540) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '不符合项依据',
  `correctActivity` varchar(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '纠正活动',
  `workCategory` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '不符合工作类型',
  `evaluation` varchar(36) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '不符合项评价',
  `inconformityOrg` varchar(270) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '不符合工作发生部门',
  `findUser` varchar(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发现人',
  `findTime` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发现时间',
  `orderNo`  varchar(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编号',
  `correctAction` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '拟采取的纠正措施',
  `completeTime` datetime  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '拟完成时间',
  `responsibleUser` varchar(60) CHARACTER SET utf8 COLLATE utf8_general_ci NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '负责人',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
   PRIMARY KEY (`id`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE = InnoDB CHARACTER SET = utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

-- ----------------------------- 2019-12-09 add by zhangft--------------begin
CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_CORRECTION` (
  `ID` BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  `CORRECTIONCODE` VARCHAR(18) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '质控编号',
  `SOURCE` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '来源',
  `SOURCENO` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '来源编号',
  `FACTDESCRIPTION` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '事实描述',
  `BASIS` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '依据',
  `RESPONSIBLEORGNAME` VARCHAR(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '责任部门',
  `RESPONSIBLELEADER` VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '负责人',
  `DISCOVERDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发现时间',
  `DISCOVERUSER` VARCHAR(300) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发现人',

) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='纠正措施计划';

-- ----------------------------- 2019-12-09 add by zhengxy--------------begin
DELETE FROM T_CORE_TABLE_HIERARCHY WHERE TABLENAME = 'T_EMC_OT_ITEM_GROUP';
DELETE FROM T_CORE_TABLE_HIERARCHY WHERE TABLENAME = 'T_EMC_ORDER' AND MASTERTABLENAME = 'T_EMC_PROJECT';
COMMIT;

-- ----------------------------- 2019-12-09 add by yuanhh--------------begin
update t_emc_order_task t
set t.itemgroupid= (select itemgroupid from T_EMC_ORDER_ITEM_GROUP where ORDERID=t.ORDERID and TESTID=t.TESTID and METHODID=t.METHODID and testMethodId=t.TESTMETHODID and SCHEMENAME=t.SCHEMENAME limit 0,1),
t.itemgroup=(select itemgroup from T_EMC_ORDER_ITEM_GROUP where ORDERID=t.ORDERID and TESTID=t.TESTID and METHODID=t.METHODID and testMethodId=t.TESTMETHODID and SCHEMENAME=t.SCHEMENAME limit 0,1);
COMMIT;

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_SOL`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `SOLNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液名称',
     `SOLSOURCE` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液来源',
     `SOLNO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液编号',
     `SOLCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '溶液类别',
     `CALUCATE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否参与计算',
     `PARENTSOLNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '上级标准物质/溶液',
     `TAKEWEIGHTORVOLUME` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '取用重量/体积',
     `PARTAKEUSERID` VARCHAR(360)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '共同完成人',
     `PARTAKEUSERNAME` VARCHAR(600)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '共同完成人',
     `PREPAREUSERID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '配制人',
     `TABCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '页签类型',
     `CALIBRATEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标定日期',
     `CORRECTFACTOR` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '校正系数K',
     `REAGENTNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '试剂名称',
     `REAGENTLEVEL` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '试剂等级',
     `REAGENTMANUFACTOR` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '试剂生产厂家及编号',
     `BALANCEMODEL` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '称量天平型号及编号',
     `DRYTEMPERATURE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '干燥温度',
     `REAGENTWEIGHT` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '试剂重量',
     `SOLVENTNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '溶剂名称',
     `CONSTANTVOLUME` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '定容体积',
     `PREPARETEMPERATURE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '配制温度',
     `PREPARECONCENT` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '配制浓度',
     `CALIBRATESOLNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标定用标液名称',
     `CALIBRATESOLCONCENT` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标定用标液浓度',
     `TOCALIBRATESOLNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '待标定溶液名称',
     `TOCALIBRATESOLVOLUME` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '待标定溶液体积',
     `BURETTEVOLUME` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '滴定管体积',
     `CALIBRATEDCONCENT` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标定后溶液浓度',
     `ORIGINALCONCENT` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液原浓度',
     `TRANSFERVOLUME` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液移取体积',
     `DILUTIONNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '稀释介质名称',
     `PREPARECONSTANTVOLUME` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液配制定容体积',
     `PREPAREDCONCENT` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液配制后浓度',
     `PREPAREDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '配制日期',
     `SOLPREPAREDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液配制日期',
     `VOLUMEUNITNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '体积单位',
     `CONCENTUNITNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '浓度单位',
     `WEIGHTUNITNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '重量单位',
     `VALIDDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '失效期',
     `SCRIPT` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '计算公式',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='标准溶液配制';
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_SOL_RECORD`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `SOLID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '标准溶液ID',
     `TITRATIONNUM` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '滴定次数',
     `FINALVALUE` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '终读',
     `STARTVALUE` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '始读',
     `CONSUMEVALUE` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '消耗',
     `UNITNAME` VARCHAR(64)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '单位',
     `ORDERCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品类型',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='标定原始记录';

     `PREPARECONCENT` DECIMAL(15,6)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '配制浓度',

     `VALIDDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '失效期',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
 PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='一般试剂配制';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_COMPLAINT`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `QUALITYCONTROLNO` VARCHAR(18) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '质控编号',
     `COMPLAINTORG` VARCHAR(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '投诉单位',
     `COMPLAINTUSER` VARCHAR(240) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '投诉人',
     `COMPLAINTMETHOD` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '投诉方式',
     `COMPLAINTTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '投诉时间',
     `ORDERNO` VARCHAR(18) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '样品编号',
     `CLASSIFICATION` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分类情况',
     `COMPLAINTCONTENT` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '投诉内容',
     `SOLVEPROBLEM` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '要解决的问题',
     `OTHERDATA` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '其他资料',
     `RESPONSIBLELEADER` VARCHAR(240) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '负责人',
     `INPUTUSER` VARCHAR(240) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '录入人',
     `SURVEYMEMBER` VARCHAR(240) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调查组成员',
     `SURVEYSTARTTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调查开始时间',
     `SURVEYENDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调查结束时间',
     `SURVEYMETHOD` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调查方式',
     `SURVEYRESULT` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '调查结果',
     `PROCESSRESULT` VARCHAR(540) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '处理结果',

     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
     PRIMARY KEY(ID)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='申投诉申请';

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `RELATECODE` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '关联代码',
     `RELATENAME` VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '关联名称',
     `RELATESCRIPT` VARCHAR(1080) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '关联公式',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='数据相关性表';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION PRIMARY KEY (ID);

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM`(
    `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `CORRELATIONID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '相关性编码',
     `TESTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试代码',
     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法编码',
     `ITEMID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项目',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称'
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='数据相关性分析项';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM ADD CONSTRAINT PK_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM PRIMARY KEY (ID);
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_DCI_CORRELATIONID ON T_EMC_DATA_CORRELATION_ITEM (CORRELATIONID);

-- ----------------------------- 2020-02-19 add by magt----------------begin
ALTER TABLE `T_EMC_EQUIPT_TEST_METHOD`
ADD COLUMN `ANALYSISCATEGORY` VARCHAR(36) COMMENT '分析类型' AFTER `METHODID`,
ADD COLUMN `DEFAULTFLAG` VARCHAR(36) COMMENT '是否默认' AFTER `ANALYSISCATEGORY`;
INSERT INTO T_EMC_EQUIPT_TEST_METHOD
  (ID,
   TESTMETHODID,
   ANALYSISCATEGORY,
   EQUIPTID,
   TESTCATEGORYID,
   TESTID,
   METHODID,
   DEFAULTFLAG,
   CREATEDBYID,
   CREATEDBYNAME,
   CREATEDTIME,
   CREATEDBYORGID,
   CREATEDBYORGNAME)
  SELECT T.ID,
         T.TESTMETHODID,
         T.ANALYSISCATEGORY,
         T.EQUIPTID,
         T.TESTCATEGORYID,
         T.TESTID,
         T.METHODID,
         T.DEFAULTFLAG,
         T.CREATEDBYID,
         T.CREATEDBYNAME,
         T.CREATEDTIME,
         T.CREATEDBYORGID,
         T.CREATEDBYORGNAME
    FROM (SELECT A.ID,
                 A.TESTMETHODID,
                 A.ANALYSISCATEGORY,
                 A.EQUIPTID,
                 A.DEFAULTFLAG,
                 A.CREATEDBYID,
                 A.CREATEDBYNAME,
                 A.CREATEDTIME,
                 A.CREATEDBYORGID,
                 A.CREATEDBYORGNAME,
                 B.TESTCATEGORYID,
                 B.TESTID,
                 B.METHODID
            FROM T_EMC_TEST_METHOD_EQUIPT A
            LEFT JOIN T_EMC_TEST_METHOD B
              ON A.TESTMETHODID = B.ID) T;
COMMIT;

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_PROJECT ADD COLUMN SAMPLE VARCHAR(3) COMMENT '是否采样';
ALTER TABLE T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY ADD COLUMN SHOWSAMPLE VARCHAR(3) COMMENT '显示是否采样';
UPDATE T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY set SHOWSAMPLE = '0'  where SHOWSAMPLE IS NULL;
UPDATE T_EMC_PROJECT A LEFT JOIN T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY B ON A.BIZCATEGORYID=B.ID SET A.SAMPLE=B.SAMPLE ;
COMMIT;
-- ----------------------------- 2020-2-27 add by magh--------------end

-- ----------------------------- 2020-3-3 add by magt-- ------------------begin
-- ------------------处理现场采样历史数据------------------ ------------------
UPDATE T_EMC_SAMPLE T
   SET T.STATUS = 'draft'
 WHERE T.PROCESSSTATUS LIKE 'draft%'
   AND T.STATUS = 'toaccept'
   AND (T.PROJID IS NULL OR EXISTS
        (SELECT 1
           FROM T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
          WHERE EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
            AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID));
-- ------------------处理采样互审历史数据------------------ ------------------
UPDATE T_EMC_SAMPLE A
   SET A.STATUS = 'approve'
 WHERE (A.STATUS <> 'reportundo')
   AND (A.PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('approve', '%'))
   AND (A.PROJID IS NULL OR EXISTS
        (SELECT 1
           FROM T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
          WHERE EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
            AND EP_.ID = A.PROJID))
   AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
          FROM T_CORE_BPMN_INST           I,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK      K,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_ROLE R,
               T_CORE_ROLE_USER           U
         WHERE I.ID = K.INSTID
           AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_SAMPLE$', A.ID)
           AND K.ID = R.INSTTASKID
           AND R.ROLEID = U.ROLEID
           AND K.STATUSCODE = 'mutual'
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 1
          FROM T_CORE_BPMN_INST           I,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK      K,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_USER U
         WHERE I.ID = K.INSTID
           AND K.ID = U.INSTTASKID
           AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_SAMPLE$', A.ID)
           AND K.STATUSCODE = 'mutual');
-- ------------------处理采样审核历史数据------------------ ------------------
UPDATE T_EMC_SAMPLE T
   SET T.STATUS = 'audit'
 WHERE (STATUS <> 'reportundo')
   AND (PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('approve', '%'))
   AND (T.PROJID IS NULL OR EXISTS
        (SELECT 1
           FROM T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
          WHERE EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
            AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID))
   AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
          FROM T_CORE_BPMN_INST           I,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK      K,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_ROLE R,
               T_CORE_ROLE_USER           U
         WHERE I.ID = K.INSTID
           AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_SAMPLE$', T.ID)
           AND K.ID = R.INSTTASKID
           AND R.ROLEID = U.ROLEID
           AND K.STATUSCODE = 'audit'
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 1
          FROM T_CORE_BPMN_INST           I,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK      K,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_USER U
         WHERE I.ID = K.INSTID
           AND K.ID = U.INSTTASKID
           AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_SAMPLE$', T.ID)
           AND K.STATUSCODE = 'audit');
-- ------------------处理样品接收历史数据------------------ ------------------
UPDATE T_EMC_SAMPLE T
   SET T.STATUS = 'preLogged'
 WHERE (STATUS = 'toaccept')
   AND (PROCESSSTATUS = 'done')
   AND (T.PROJID IS NULL OR EXISTS
        (SELECT 1
           FROM T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
          WHERE EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
            AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID));
-- ------------------处理样品接收提交后历史数据------------------ ------------------
UPDATE T_EMC_SAMPLE T
   SET T.STATUS = 'done'
 WHERE (T.STATUS = 'accepted')
   AND (T.PROCESSSTATUS = 'done')
   AND (T.PROJID IS NULL OR EXISTS
        (SELECT 1
           FROM T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
          WHERE EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
            AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID));
COMMIT;
-- ----------------------------- 2020-3-3 add by magt--------------end

-- ----------------------------- 2020-3-6 add by magt--------------begin

-- -- - -----------处理样品接收order_task表历史数据---------------
UPDATE T_EMC_ORDER_TASK A LEFT JOIN T_EMC_ORDER B ON A.ORDERID = B.ID LEFT JOIN T_EMC_SAMPLE T ON B.SAMPLEID = T.ID
   SET A.STATUS = 'accepted'
 WHERE (t. STATUS = 'preLogged')
   AND (t.PROCESSSTATUS = 'done')
   AND (T.PROJID IS NULL OR EXISTS
        (SELECT 1
           FROM T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
          WHERE EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
            AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID));

-- -- - -----------处理分析校核order_task表历史数据---------------
UPDATE T_EMC_ORDER_TASK A LEFT JOIN T_EMC_RUN T ON A.RUNID = T.ID
   SET A.STATUS = 'mutualAudited'
 WHERE (T.PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('approve', '%'))
   AND EXISTS (SELECT 1
          FROM T_CORE_BPMN_INST           I,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK      K,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_ROLE R,
               T_CORE_ROLE_USER           U
         WHERE I.ID = K.INSTID
           AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_RUN$', T.ID)
           AND K.ID = R.INSTTASKID
           AND R.ROLEID = U.ROLEID
        UNION ALL
        SELECT 1
          FROM T_CORE_BPMN_INST           I,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK      K,
               T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_USER U
         WHERE I.ID = K.INSTID
           AND K.ID = U.INSTTASKID
           AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_RUN$', T.ID));

-- ----------------------------- 2020-3-6 add by magt--------------end

     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `ENDDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '到期日期',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `ACTIVATEDFLAG` VARCHAR(1)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用标志',
     `ACTIVATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人ID',
     `ACTIVATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
     `ACTIVATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='附件信息表';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_FETCH_DATA (
  ID BIGINT(20) NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
  TABLENAME VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '记录表名称',
  FIELDONE BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '名称1数量',
  FIELDTWO BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '名称2数量',
  FIELDTHREE BIGINT(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '名称3数量',
  FIELDONENAME VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '名称1',
  FIELDTWONAME VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '名称2',
  FIELDTHREENAME VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '名称3',
  CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
  CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  ACTIVATEDFLAG VARCHAR(1) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用标志',
  ACTIVATEDBYID VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人编码',
  ACTIVATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
  ACTIVATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
  PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='生物查表';

  CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(255) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='生物查表明细';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS T_EMC_REPORT_TMPL(
     ID BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     TMPLNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '模板名称',
     REMARK VARCHAR(540)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     ORDERNO INT(18)  NOT NULL COMMENT '排序码',
     CREATEDBYID varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
     CREATEDBYNAME varchar(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     CREATEDTIME datetime  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
     CREATEDBYORGID varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     CREATEDBYORGNAME varchar(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     ACTIVATEDFLAG varchar(1) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用标志',
     ACTIVATEDBYID varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人编码',
     ACTIVATEDBYNAME varchar(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
     ACTIVATEDTIME datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
      PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='例行上报模板配置表';

     ID BIGINT  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     REPORTTMPLID BIGINT(18)  NOT NULL COMMENT '上报模板id',
     REPORTNAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '上报名称',
     MONITORTMPLID VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测模板id',
     SCHEMENAME VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方案名称',
     TESTID BIGINT(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试id',
     ITEMID BIGINT(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项id',
     ITEMALIASNAME VARCHAR(120)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项别名',
     ORDERNO INT(18)  NOT NULL COMMENT '排序码',
     CREATEDBYID varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
     CREATEDBYNAME varchar(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     CREATEDTIME datetime  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
     CREATEDBYORGID varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     CREATEDBYORGNAME varchar(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='例行上报模板配置详情表';

-- ----------------------------- 2020-3-21 add by magt--------------begin

ALTER TABLE T_EMC_RUN ADD COLUMN STATUS VARCHAR(36) NULL COMMENT '状态';
COMMIT;
-- ----------------------------- 2020-3-21 add by magt--------------end

-- ----------------------------- 2020-3-21 add by magt--------------------------------begin
-- ----------------------------- 处理历史数据--------------
-- ----------------------------- 项目登记--------------
UPDATE T_EMC_PROJECT A
SET A. STATUS = 'create'
WHERE
    A.PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('draft', '%')
AND A. STATUS <> 'cancel'
AND A.SOURCE IS NULL
OR A.SOURCE = '';

-- ----------------------------- 方案编制--------------

UPDATE T_EMC_PROJECT T
SET STATUS = 'schemeDraft'
WHERE
    T.PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('approve', '%')
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT
        1
    FROM
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_ROLE R,
        T_CORE_ROLE_USER U
    WHERE
        I.ID = K.INSTID
    AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_PROJECT$', T.ID)
    AND K.ID = R.INSTTASKID
    AND R.ROLEID = U.ROLEID
    AND K.STATUSCODE = 'schemeedit'
    UNION ALL
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_USER U
        WHERE
            I.ID = K.INSTID
        AND K.ID = U.INSTTASKID
        AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_PROJECT$', T.ID)
        AND K.STATUSCODE = 'schemeedit'
)
AND T. STATUS <> 'cancel';

-- ----------------------------- 方案审核--------------

UPDATE
    T_EMC_PROJECT T SET STATUS  = 'schemeAudit'
WHERE
    PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('approve', '%')
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT
        1
    FROM
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_ROLE R,
        T_CORE_ROLE_USER U
    WHERE
        I.ID = K.INSTID
    AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_PROJECT$', T.ID)
    AND K.ID = R.INSTTASKID
    AND R.ROLEID = U.ROLEID
    AND K.STATUSCODE = 'schemeaudit'
    UNION ALL
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_USER U
        WHERE
            I.ID = K.INSTID
        AND K.ID = U.INSTTASKID
        AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_PROJECT$', T.ID)
        AND K.STATUSCODE = 'schemeaudit'
)
AND T. STATUS <> 'cancel';

-- ----------------------------- 采样安排--------------

UPDATE
    T_EMC_FOLDER T SET T.STATUS = 'draft'
WHERE
    SOURCE = 'monitorscheme'
AND PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('draft', '%')
AND (
    T.PROJID IS NULL
    OR EXISTS (
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
        WHERE
            EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
        AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID
    )
);

-- ----------------------------- 现场采样-采样准备--------------

UPDATE T_EMC_PROJECT T
SET STATUS = 'auditFinished'
WHERE
    T. STATUS = 'normal'
AND T.PROCESSSTATUS = 'done'
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT
        1
    FROM
        T_EMC_ORDER O,
        T_EMC_FOLDER F
    WHERE
        O.FOLDERID = F.ID
    AND O.PROJID = T.ID
    AND F.PROCESSSTATUS = 'done'
    AND F. STATUS = 'tosample'
)
AND (
    EXISTS (
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_EMC_SAMPLE A
        WHERE
            A.PROJID = T.ID
        AND A.PROCESSSTATUS LIKE 'draft%'
        AND A. STATUS != 'reportundo'
    )
    OR EXISTS (
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_EMC_ORDER O
        WHERE
            O.PROJID = T.ID
        AND O.SAMPLEID IS NULL
    )
);

UPDATE
    T_EMC_FOLDER T SET T.STATUS = 'draw'
WHERE

(`STATUS` = 'tosample')
AND (`PROCESSSTATUS` = 'done')
AND (
    T.PROJID IS NULL
    OR EXISTS (
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
        WHERE
            EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
        AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID
    )
);

-- ----------------------------- 分析录入--------------

UPDATE
    T_EMC_ORDER_TASK T
LEFT JOIN T_EMC_PROJECT P ON T.PROJID = P.ID
SET T.STATUS = 'Logged'
WHERE
    (
        P. STATUS != 'cancle'
        OR P. STATUS IS NULL
    )
AND T. STATUS = 'preLogged'
AND ANALYSISTYPE = 'analysis'
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT
        1
    FROM
        T_CORE_USER_PERMISSION UP_
    WHERE
        UP_.PERMISSIONTYPEID = 'T_CORE_ORG'
    AND UP_.TARGETID = T.ANALYSISORGID
)
AND (RUNID IS NULL OR RUNID = '');

-- ----------------------------- 分析校核：--------------

UPDATE
    T_EMC_RUN T SET T.STATUS = 'approve'
WHERE
    PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('approve', '%')
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT
        1
    FROM
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_ROLE R,
        T_CORE_ROLE_USER U
    WHERE
        I.ID = K.INSTID
    AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_RUN$', T.ID)
    AND K.ID = R.INSTTASKID
    AND R.ROLEID = U.ROLEID
    UNION ALL
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_USER U
        WHERE
            I.ID = K.INSTID
        AND K.ID = U.INSTTASKID
        AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_RUN$', T.ID)
);

UPDATE T_EMC_ORDER_TASK A
LEFT JOIN T_EMC_RUN T ON A.RUNID = T.ID
SET A.STATUS = 'approve'
WHERE
    T.PROCESSSTATUS LIKE CONCAT('approve', '%')
AND EXISTS (
    SELECT
        1
    FROM
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
        T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_ROLE R,
        T_CORE_ROLE_USER U
    WHERE
        I.ID = K.INSTID
    AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_RUN$', T.ID)
    AND K.ID = R.INSTTASKID
    AND R.ROLEID = U.ROLEID
    UNION ALL
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST I,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK K,
            T_CORE_BPMN_INST_TASK_USER U
        WHERE
            I.ID = K.INSTID
        AND K.ID = U.INSTTASKID
        AND I.TARGETID = CONCAT('T_EMC_RUN$', T.ID)
);

UPDATE T_EMC_ORDER_TASK T SET T.STATUS = 'audit' WHERE T.STATUS = 'released';

-- ----------------------------- 监测项目修改--------------

UPDATE T_EMC_ORDER_TASK T
SET T.STATUS = 'Logged'
WHERE
    T. STATUS = 'preLogged'
AND T.ANALYSISTYPE = 'analysis'
AND (
    T.PROJID IS NULL
    OR EXISTS (
        SELECT
            1
        FROM
            T_EMC_PROJECT EP_
        WHERE
            EP_. STATUS <> 'cancel'
        AND EP_.ID = T.PROJID
    )
);

CREATE TABLE `T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT` (
  `ID` bigint(30) NOT NULL COMMENT 'ID',
  `BIZCATEGORYID` bigint(30) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务类型ID',
  `PROJNAME` varchar(120) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '项目名称',
  `ACTIVATEDFLAG` varchar(3) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否启用',
  `ACTIVATEDBYID` varchar(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人编码',
  `ACTIVATEDBYNAME` varchar(72) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人名称',
  `ACTIVATEDTIME` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
  `CREATEDBYID` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  `CREATEDBYNAME` varchar(72) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  `CREATEDTIME` datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  `CREATEDBYORGID` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
  `CREATEDBYORGNAME` varchar(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`) USING BTREE ,
  INDEX `IDX_BIZCATEGORYID` (`BIZCATEGORYID`) USING BTREE
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '业务类型常用项目表' ROW_FORMAT = Dynamic;

INSERT INTO `T_CORE_TABLE` (`ID`, `AUDITABLE`, `ATTACHMENT`) VALUES ('T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT', '0', '0');

INSERT INTO `T_CORE_TABLE_HIERARCHY`(`ID`, `TABLENAME`, `MASTERTABLENAME`, `REFERCOLUMN`)
VALUES ('100510','T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY_PROJECT', 'T_EMC_BIZ_CATEGORY', 'BIZCATEGORYID');

  FILENO varchar(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件编号',

  CREATEDBYID varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人ID',
  CREATEDBYNAME varchar(72) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
  CREATEDTIME datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单日期',
  CREATEDBYORGID varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',

) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COLLATE = utf8_general_ci COMMENT = '文件申请表' ;

-- ----------------------------- 2020-04-10 add by jinx--------------begin

-- 建表语句

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_CONTRACT`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `ENTRUSTORG` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '委托单位',
     `CONTRACTNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同名称',
     `CONTRACTNO` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同编号',
     `TOTALPRICE` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '总价',
     `CONTRACESOURCE` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同来源',
     `MONITORPURPOSE` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测目的',
     `MONITORORDER` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测样品',
     `CMAF` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '资质认定',
     `CONTRACTREPORT` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报告',
     `OTHERINFO` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '其他',
     `REVIEWER` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审人员',
     `REVIEWDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审日期',
     `REVIEWADDRESS` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审地点',
     `BEGINDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同开始日期',
     `ENDDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同结束日期',
     `REVIEWOPINION` VARCHAR(600)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '评审组意见',
     `YEAR` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同年份',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='合同表';


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_REVIEW_CONTENT`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `CONTRACTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同ID',
     `CONTENTNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '名称',
     `SATISFIED` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否满足',
     `EXPLAIN` VARCHAR(600)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '说明',
     `CONTENTTYPE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '提要类型',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='评审内容提要表';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_REVIEW_CONTENT_CONTRACTID ON T_EMC_REVIEW_CONTENT (CONTRACTID);


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_EXPENSE_CATEGORY`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `CONTRACTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同ID',
     `EXPENSECATEGORY` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '费用类型',
     `PRICETERM` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '报价方式',
     `OTHEREXPENSE` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '其他费用',
     `TOTALPRICE` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '总价',
     `ORIGINALREPORT` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '正本报告份数',
     `DUPLICATEREPORT` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '副本报告份数',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='费用类别表';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_EXPENSE_CATEGORY_CONTRACTID ON T_EMC_EXPENSE_CATEGORY (CONTRACTID);


CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_MONITOR_ITEM`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `EXPENSECATEGORYID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '费用类别ID',
     `CONTRACTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '合同ID',
     `TESTID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试ID',
     `METHODID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '方法ID',
     `MONITORPOINTCOUNTONE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点数1',
     `SAMPLECOUNTONE` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样次数1',
     `MONITORPOINTCOUNTTWO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测点数2',
     `SAMPLECOUNTTWO` VARCHAR(18)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采样次数2',
     `PRICE` VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '价格',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='监测项目表';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_MONITOR_ITEM_EXPENSECATEGORYID ON T_EMC_MONITOR_ITEM (EXPENSECATEGORYID);

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_CALI_RESULT (
    ID bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '校准结果确认记录id',
    EQUIPTID varchar(18) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '站内编号',
    EQUIPTNAME varchar(128) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '仪器名称',
    SPECIFICATION varchar(32) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '规格型号',
    CYCLE varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '校准周期',
    UNIT varchar(60) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '校准单位',
    VALIDTIME datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '有效期',
    REPORTCATEGORY varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书报告性质',
    CERTNO varchar(32) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '证书报告编号',
    CALIREASON varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '校准依据',
    CALIRESULT varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '校准结果',
    CLAIM varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否满足要求',
    UNCERTAINTY varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '校准结果不确定度',
    CONTENTRESULT varchar(128) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '根据证书、报告内容确认',
    REVIEWER varchar(30) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核人',
    REVIEWDATE datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核日期',
    PROCESSSTATUS varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
    PRIMARY KEY (ID)
)ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT = '校准结果确认记录';

CREATE TABLE T_EMC_EQUIPT_CALI_RESULT_NORM (
    ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '依据标准id',
    TESTID BIGINT NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试编码',

    CHECKTIME datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '最近检定时间',
    EQUIPTMENT varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检查所用仪器设备',
    CHECKREASON varchar(128) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '核查依据',
    CHECKWAY varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '核查方式',
    CHECKPROCESS varchar(360) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '核查过程记录',
    CHECKRESULT varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '核查结果',
    CHECKCONCLUSION varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '核查结论',
    OPINION varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '处理意见',
    RECORDER varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '记录人',
    RECORDERDATE datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '记录日期',
    DIRECTOR varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '技术主管',
    DIRECTORDATE datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '技术主管日期',
    PRINCIPAL varchar(66) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '技术负责人',
    PRINCIPALDATE datetime NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '技术负责人日期',
    PROCESSSTATUS varchar(36) NULL DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
    PRIMARY KEY (ID)
)ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT = '仪器期间核查记录';

-- 文件修改表
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_FILE_MODIFY (
    ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
    FILEID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件编号',
    FILENAME VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '文件名称',
    MODIFYINGCONTENT VARCHAR(1200) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '欲修改内容',
    MODIFIEDCONTENT VARCHAR(1200) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改后内容',
    MODIFYREASON VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修改原因',
    IMPLEMENTDATE DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '实施日期',
    AUDITORID  VARCHAR(36) NULL COMMENT '审核人编码',
    AUDITORNAME  VARCHAR(60) NULL COMMENT '审核人名称',
    CONFIRMERID VARCHAR(36) NULL COMMENT '确认人编码',
    CONFIRMERNAME VARCHAR(60) NULL COMMENT '确认人名称',
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
    CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
    PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=UTF8 COMMENT='文件修改表';

-- 采购单表
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_PURCH (
    ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
    PURCHASECATEGORY VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采购品类别',
    INSTRUCTION VARCHAR(1200) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '说明',
    REMARK VARCHAR(360) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
    CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
    PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=UTF8 COMMENT='采购单表';

INSERT INTO T_CORE_TABLE (ID,AUDITABLE,ATTACHMENT) VALUES ('T_EMC_PURCH', '1', '1');

-- 采购明细表
CREATE TABLE T_EMC_PURCH_DETAIL (
    ID BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
    PURCHID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采购单ID',
    PURCHASENAME VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '采购品名称',
    SPECIFICATION VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '规格',
    TECHNICALINDEX VARCHAR(360) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '技术指标及要求',
    SUPPLIER VARCHAR(66) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '供应商',
    NUMB BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '数量',
    REMARK VARCHAR(360) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
    AUDITORID VARCHAR(36) NULL COMMENT '审核人编码',
    CONFIRMERID VARCHAR(36) NULL COMMENT '确认人编码',
    AUDITORNAME  VARCHAR(60) NULL COMMENT '审核人名称',
    CONFIRMERNAME VARCHAR(60) NULL COMMENT '确认人名称',
    PROCESSSTATUS VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
    CREATEDBYNAME VARCHAR(60) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
    CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
    PRIMARY KEY (ID)
) ENGINE=INNODB DEFAULT CHARSET=UTF8 COMMENT='采购明细表';

-- ----------------------------- 2021-03-29 add by xiongjie--------------begin
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_QUOTATION_ADDPRICE`(
     `ID` BIGINT(20)  NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `PRICENAME` VARCHAR(150)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '费用名称',

    ID bigint NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
    TESTMETHODID bigint DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法Id',
    ORGID VARCHAR(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '机构Id',
    CREATEDBYID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',

    CREATEDTIME DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
    CREATEDBYORGID VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
    CREATEDBYORGNAME VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
	PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
 )ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='监测能力库';

(
  id               bigint not null COMMENT '主键',
  testcapacityid   bigint DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '监测能力库ID',

  activatedbyid    VARCHAR(32) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人编码',
  activatedbyname  VARCHAR(64) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用人姓名',
  activatedtime    DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '启用时间',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
)ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='监测能力库分析项组';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_INVOICE`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `SYSNO` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '系统流水号',
     `INVOICENAME` VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发票名称',
     `INVOICENO` VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发票编号',
     `INVOICECATEGORY` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '发票类型',
     `MAKEDATE` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开票时间',
     `DRAWERID` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开票人id',
     `DRAWER` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开票人',
     `INVOICEAMOUNT` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开票金额',
     `CONTRACTID` VARCHAR(300) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '关联合同id',
     `CONTRACTNAME` VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '关联合同',
     `PROJECTID` VARCHAR(300) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '关联项目id',
     `PROJECTNAME` VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '关联项目',
     `DONEFLAG` VARCHAR(2)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否回款完成',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `AUDITUSERID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核人id',
     `AUDITUSER` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '审核人',
     `PROCESSSTATUS` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '流程状态',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='开票记录';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_RETURNED`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `INVOICEID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开票记录id',
     `INVOICEAMOUNT` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '开票金额',
     `RETURNEDAMOUNT` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '本次回款金额',
     `UNCOLLECTEDAMOUNT` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '未回款金额',
     `MAKEUSERID` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '回款人id',
     `MAKEUSER` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '回款人',
     `MAKETIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '回款时间',
     `REMARK` VARCHAR(300)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '备注',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='回款记录';

CREATE TABLE `t_emc_qc_item` (
  `ID` bigint(20) NOT NULL COMMENT 'ID主键',
  `TESTMETHODID` bigint(20) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '测试方法ID',
  `ITEMNAME` varchar(180) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项名称',
  `ITEMALIASNAME` varchar(180) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '分析项别名',
  `LOWLIMIT` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出低限',
  `HIGHLIMIT` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '检出高限',
  `REVISIONRULE` varchar(36) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '修约规则',
  `QCCATEGORYID` varchar(400) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC中登录',
  `QCCATEGORYNAME` varchar(600) DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'QC类型名称(多个)',
  PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8;

-- 建表语句
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_SEAL_RULE`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `NAME` VARCHAR(90)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '规则名称',
     `FIELD` VARCHAR(90)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '领域',
     `BUSINESSCATEGORY` VARCHAR(90)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '业务类型',
     `LABORATORY` VARCHAR(150)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '实验室',
     `ACTIVEDFLAG` VARCHAR(3)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '是否启用',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='盖章打印规则';

CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS `T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS`(
     `ID` BIGINT NOT NULL COMMENT '主键',
     `SEALRULEID` BIGINT DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '盖章打印规则id',
     `SEALGROUPNO` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '图章组编码',
     `SEALGROUPNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '图章组名称',
     `SEALNO` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '图章编码',
     `SEALNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '图章名称',
     `PAGETYPE` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '页码类型',
     `FIXEDPAGENUM` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '固定页码值',
     `CUSTOMPAGENUM` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '自定义页码值',
     `XVALUE` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'X坐标',
     `YVALUE` VARCHAR(30)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT 'Y坐标',
     `CREATEDBYID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人编码',
     `CREATEDBYNAME` VARCHAR(60)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人名称',
     `CREATEDTIME` DATETIME DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单时间',
     `CREATEDBYORGID` VARCHAR(36)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位编码',
     `CREATEDBYORGNAME` VARCHAR(270)  DEFAULT NULL COMMENT '制单人单位名称',
      PRIMARY KEY (`ID`)
) ENGINE=InnoDB DEFAULT CHARSET=utf8 COMMENT='盖章打印规则详情';
CREATE INDEX IDX_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS_SEALRULEID ON T_EMC_SEAL_RULE_DETAILS (SEALRULEID);
